--- CBR-CDB ---
When patient 10021312 had the first void output since 09/16/2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT outputevents.charttime FROM outputevents WHERE outputevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10021312 ) ) AND outputevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.abbreviation = 'urine output' AND d_items.linksto = 'outputevents' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime) >= '2100-09-16' ORDER BY outputevents.charttime ASC LIMIT 1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id', 'outputevents.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_items', 'icustays', 'outputevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10021312'], ['d_items.abbreviation', '=', 'urine output'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'outputevents'], ["strftime('%y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100-09-16']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT outputevents.charttime FROM outputevents WHERE outputevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10021312 ) ) AND outputevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'void' AND d_items.linksto = 'outputevents' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime) >= '2100-09-16' ORDER BY outputevents.charttime ASC LIMIT 1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id', 'outputevents.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_items', 'icustays', 'outputevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10021312'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'void'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'outputevents'], ["strftime('%y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100-09-16']]}
----------

How many patients in 2100 received central venous catheter placement with guidance after the reopening of recent thoracotomy site procedure within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM (SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site') AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100') AS T1 JOIN (SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'central venous catheter placement with guidance') AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100') AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'central venous catheter placement with guidance' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

What were the four most frequently performed lab tests since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_labitems.label FROM d_labitems WHERE d_labitems.itemid IN ( SELECT T1.itemid FROM ( SELECT labevents.itemid, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM labevents WHERE datetime(labevents.charttime) >= datetime('now', '-1 year') GROUP BY labevents.itemid ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 4 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['d_labitems.label', 'labevents.itemid', 't1.itemid'], 'tab': ['d_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['datetime(labevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_labitems.label FROM d_labitems WHERE d_labitems.itemid IN ( SELECT T1.itemid FROM ( SELECT labevents.itemid, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM labevents WHERE datetime(labevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') GROUP BY labevents.itemid ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 4 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['d_labitems.label', 'labevents.itemid', 't1.itemid'], 'tab': ['d_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['datetime(labevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '4']]}

How many patients went through a blood (toxo) microbiology test since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT microbiologyevents.subject_id) FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['microbiologyevents.subject_id'], 'tab': ['microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT admissions.subject_id ) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT microbiologyevents.hadm_id FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'microbiologyevents.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}

Has there been any organism found in the last blood (toxo) microbiology test patient 10038081 received on their last hospital encounter?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(*) > 0 FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.hadm_id = ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038081 ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1 ) AND microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND microbiologyevents.org_name IS NOT NULL AND microbiologyevents.charttime = ( SELECT MAX(microbiologyevents.charttime) FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.hadm_id = ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038081 ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1 ) AND microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'microbiologyevents.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10038081'], ['microbiologyevents.org_name', 'is', 'not null'], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT(*)>0 FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038081 AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1 ) AND microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND microbiologyevents.org_name IS NOT NULL AND microbiologyevents.charttime = ( SELECT DISTINCT microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038081 AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1 ) AND microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND microbiologyevents.org_name IS NOT NULL ORDER BY microbiologyevents.charttime DESC LIMIT 1 )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'microbiologyevents.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10038081'], ['microbiologyevents.org_name', 'is', 'not null'], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}

List the top five most frequent procedures that patients underwent within the same month after being diagnosed with diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+1 month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What was the ciprofloxacin hcl dosage that patient 10019172 had been prescribed in 11/2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT prescriptions.dose_val_rx FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10019172 ) AND prescriptions.drug = 'ciprofloxacin hcl' AND strftime('%Y-%m',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100-11' AND prescriptions.starttime = ( SELECT DISTINCT prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10019172 ) AND prescriptions.drug = 'ciprofloxacin hcl' AND strftime('%Y-%m',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100-11' ORDER BY prescriptions.starttime ASC LIMIT 1 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.dose_val_rx', 'prescriptions.starttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10019172'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'ciprofloxacin hcl'], ["strftime('%y-%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100-11']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT SUM(prescriptions.dose_val_rx) FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10019172 ) AND prescriptions.drug = 'ciprofloxacin hcl' AND strftime('%Y-%m',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100-11'
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.dose_val_rx'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10019172'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'ciprofloxacin hcl'], ["strftime('%y-%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100-11']]}

How much does patient 10007058 change in SpO2 second measured on the first ICU visit compared to the first value measured on the first ICU visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT ( SELECT chartevents.valuenum FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058 ) AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.abbreviation = 'spo2' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) AND chartevents.charttime = ( SELECT DISTINCT chartevents.charttime FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058 ) AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.abbreviation = 'spo2' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC LIMIT 1 OFFSET 1 ) ) - ( SELECT chartevents.valuenum FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058 ) AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.abbreviation = 'spo2' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) AND chartevents.charttime = ( SELECT DISTINCT chartevents.charttime FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058 ) AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.abbreviation = 'spo2' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC LIMIT 1 ) )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'chartevents.charttime', 'chartevents.valuenum', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'chartevents', 'd_items', 'icustays'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10007058'], ['d_items.abbreviation', '=', 'spo2'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents'], ['icustays.outtime', 'is', 'not null']]}

What is the 6-month survival probability for patients living with a other and unspecified angina pectoris diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified angina pectoris' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

Since 1 year ago, what are the top five most common drugs prescribed in the same month to male patients aged 50s after being diagnosed with unspecified sinusitis (chronic)?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified sinusitis (chronic)' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE patients.gender = 'M' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5

What are the three most frequently ordered medications that female patients aged 60 or above were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE admissions.age >= 60 AND patients.gender = 'f' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3

What is the 3-month survival probability for patients living with a asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )

How many patients underwent reopening of recent thoracotomy site within the same month after the diagnosis with other acute postoperative pain, since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM (SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other acute postoperative pain') AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100') AS T1 JOIN (SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site') AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100') AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other acute postoperative pain'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to female patients in their 50s during the same hospital encounter after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND patients.gender = 'F'
        AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
    ) AS T2
    ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

How many patients underwent diagnosis of postprocedural fever within 2 months after being diagnosed with body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'postprocedural fever'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'postprocedural fever'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'postprocedural fever' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'postprocedural fever'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

Among the patients who were prescribed alprazolam, what are the five most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, COUNT(*) AS freq FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT hadm_id FROM prescriptions WHERE drug = 'alprazolam' AND datetime(starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year') ) AND datetime(starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year') GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ORDER BY freq DESC LIMIT 5 ) AS T1 WHERE T1.drug != 'alprazolam'
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year')"], ['t1.drug', '!=', 'alprazolam']]}

Since 1 year ago, what are the top four most commonly taken specimens for patients within 2 months after the diagnosis of encounter for immunization?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc 
FROM (
    SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'encounter for immunization'
        ) 
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime 
        FROM microbiologyevents 
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 
    ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
    AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month') 
    GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'encounter for immunization'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'encounter for immunization' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'encounter for immunization'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

How many patients died during the same hospital encounter after having been diagnosed in 2100 with varices of other sites?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'varices of other sites'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE admissions.discharge_location = 'died'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.discharge_location', '=', 'died'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'varices of other sites'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime, T1.hadm_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'varices of other sites' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND strftime('%Y',T1.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%Y',patients.dod) = '2100' ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE T2.hadm_id = T3.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.hadm_id', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'varices of other sites'], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%y',patients.dod)", '=', '2100'], ['t2.hadm_id', '=', 't3.hadm_id']]}

Among patients who were diagnosed with unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma during this year, what were the top three most common diagnoses that followed within 2 months?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title 
FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN (
    SELECT T3.icd_code 
    FROM (
        SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
        FROM (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
                SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
                FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
                WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma'
            ) 
            AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year')
        ) AS T1 
        JOIN (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year')
        ) AS T2 
        ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
        WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
        AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month') 
        GROUP BY T2.icd_code
    ) AS T3 
    WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
)
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.subject_id, prescriptions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT prescriptions.subject_id, prescriptions.hadm_id, prescriptions.drug FROM prescriptions WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id WHERE T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id AND T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4

----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

----------

TRUTH:

Could you tell me the name of the drug that was prescribed two or more times in 07/this year to patient 10020740?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT DISTINCT prescriptions.drug 
FROM prescriptions 
WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10020740
) 
AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year') 
AND strftime('%m', prescriptions.starttime) = '07' 
GROUP BY prescriptions.drug 
HAVING COUNT(prescriptions.drug) >= 2
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10020740'], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ["strftime('%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "07' ) >= 2"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, COUNT( prescriptions.starttime ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10020740 ) AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') AND strftime('%m',prescriptions.starttime) = '07' GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 >= 2
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10020740'], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["strftime('%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '07'], ['t1.c1', '>=', '2']]}

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to male patients in their 30s during the same month after they were diagnosed with hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia during this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE patients.gender = 'M' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4

TRUTH:

How many patients died during the same month after having been diagnosed since 1 year ago with acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure'
    ) 
    AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('now', '-1 year')
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.dischtime 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE admissions.discharge_location = 'died'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.dischtime, 'start of month');
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.dischtime', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.discharge_location', '=', 'died'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.dischtime, 'start of month');"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure'], ['datetime(patients.dod)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t3.dod,'start of month')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

How many patients received albuterol inhaler in the same month after the confirmation of toxic encephalopathy?
----------

What are the four most frequently ordered medications that male patients aged 20s were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with mitral valve disorders since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29
        AND patients.gender = 'M'
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2
    ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:

Among patients in their 60 or above , what are the top three prescribed drugs?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age >= 60 ) AS T2 GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.age >= 60 ) GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with history of falling?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J', patients.dod) - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS survival_rate FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON diagnoses_icd.icd_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code JOIN patients ON diagnoses_icd.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' AND patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%J', current_time) - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4;
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'history of falling'], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4;']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'history of falling'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) = strftime('%Y',CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) AND admissions.discharge_location = 'died' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.discharge_location', '=', 'died'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'facial weakness'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '=', "strftime('%y',current_timestamp)"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y',current_timestamp)"]]}

TRUTH:

Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.hadm_id', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'facial weakness'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ['t2.hadm_id', '=', 't3.hadm_id']]}

What are the top five most frequent medications prescribed within 2 months to the patients in their 50s after they were diagnosed with cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_date) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y',current_date) ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Calculate the total number of patients diagnosed with other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use who didn't revisit the hospital within the same month since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AND NOT EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM admissions AS revisit WHERE revisit.subject_id = admissions.subject_id AND strftime('%Y-%m', revisit.admittime) = strftime('%Y-%m', admissions.admittime) AND revisit.admittime > admissions.admittime )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ['revisit.admittime', '>', 'admissions.admittime'], ['revisit.subject_id', '=', 'admissions.subject_id'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y-%m',revisit.admittime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m', admissions.admittime)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}
----------

Please list the yearly volume of chest tube #1 that was output by patient 10019172 since 09/2100. If multiple chest tube #1 volumes are measured yearly, take the average.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT strftime('%Y', outputevents.charttime) AS year, AVG(outputevents.value) AS avg_volume FROM outputevents WHERE outputevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10019172 ) ) AND outputevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'chest tube #1' AND d_items.linksto = 'outputevents' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m', outputevents.charttime) >= '2100-09' GROUP BY strftime('%Y', outputevents.charttime)

For patients who underwent respiratory ventilation, 24-96 consecutive hours since 2100, what were the five most common diagnoses during the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title 
FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN (
    SELECT T3.icd_code 
    FROM (
        SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
        FROM (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
            FROM procedures_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
                SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
                FROM d_icd_procedures 
                WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'respiratory ventilation, 24-96 consecutive hours'
            ) 
            AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
        ) AS T1 
        JOIN (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
        ) AS T2 
        ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
        WHERE strftime('%m', T1.charttime) = strftime('%m', T2.charttime) 
        GROUP BY T2.icd_code
    ) AS T3 
    WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
)
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'respiratory ventilation, 24-96 consecutive hours'], ["strftime('%m',t1.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%m', t2.charttime)"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'respiratory ventilation, 24-96 consecutive hours' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'respiratory ventilation, 24-96 consecutive hours'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What are the three most frequent drugs prescribed to female patients with an age of 20s within the same month after they were diagnosed with unspecified acquired hypothyroidism?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) AND admissions.gender = 'F' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.gender = 'F' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.gender', '=', 'f'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

How many people died after being diagnosed with posttraumatic stress disorder within 2 months throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder') AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year') AND datetime(patients.dod) BETWEEN datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

What are the top three most commonly prescribed medicines in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year') GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified.
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

Among patients in their 30s since 2100, what are the top three prescribed drugs?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT prescriptions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug FROM prescriptions WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.subject_id', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 ) AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}
