What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of septic shock within 2 months during the last year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM (SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'septic shock') AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-1 year')) AS T1 JOIN (SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.dischtime FROM admissions WHERE admissions.discharge_location = 'died' AND datetime(admissions.dischtime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-1 year')) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.dischtime AND datetime(T2.dischtime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.dischtime', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.discharge_location', '=', 'died'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'septic shock'], ["datetime(admissions.dischtime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-1 year')"], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.dischtime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'septic shock' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'septic shock'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

GOLD: SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status' ) AND strftime('%J','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to female patients in their 50s during the same hospital encounter after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in this year?
PRED: SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND patients.gender = 'F' AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
GOLD: SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
PRED_OUT: Counter()
GOLD_OUT: Counter({('iso-osmotic dextrose',): 1, ('vancomycin',): 1, ('sodium chloride 0.9%  flush',): 1, ('lactated ringers',): 1, ('0.9% sodium chloride',): 1, ('0.9% sodium chloride (mini bag plus)',): 1})

GOLD: SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4 )
PRED_OUT: Counter()

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with history of falling?
PRED: SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J', patients.dod) - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS survival_rate FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON diagnoses_icd.icd_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code JOIN patients ON diagnoses_icd.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' AND patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%J', '2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4;
GOLD: SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
PRED_OUT: Counter({(None,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(1.0,): 1})


How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) = strftime('%Y',CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) AND admissions.discharge_location = 'died' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
GOLD: SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime, T1.hadm_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE T2.hadm_id = T3.hadm_id
PRED_OUT: Counter({(0,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(1,): 1})

Among those who were prescribed with pseudoephedrine, what are the three most commonly prescribed drugs at the same time in 2100?
PRED: SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT DISTINCT hadm_id FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'pseudoephedrine' AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.drug != 'pseudoephedrine' AND T1.C1 <= 3
GOLD: SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'pseudoephedrine' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
